	private void SpawnShimmeredItem(short idToCheck, bool splitToSides, int numberOfItems)
	{
		int num = Item.NewItem(GetItemSource_Misc(ItemSourceID.Shimmer), (int)position.X, (int)position.Y, width, height, idToCheck);
		WorldItem worldItem = Main.item[num];
		worldItem.stack = 1;
		worldItem.shimmerTime = 1f;
		worldItem.shimmered = true;
		worldItem.shimmerWet = true;
		worldItem.wet = true;
		worldItem.velocity *= 0.1f;
		worldItem.playerIndexTheItemIsReservedFor = Main.myPlayer;

	{
		if (inner.CanShimmer())

	{

		int transformToItem = ShimmerTransforms.GetTransformToItem(shimmerEquivalentType);

				if (stack > 1)

				stack *= 1000000;
				break;

	private static void SyncItemsInSection(int toClient, Point sectionCoordinates)
	{
		Rectangle rectangle = new Rectangle(sectionCoordinates.X * 200 * 16, sectionCoordinates.Y * 150 * 16, 3200, 2400);
		rectangle.Inflate(16, 16);
		for (int i = 0; i < 400; i++)
		{
			WorldItem worldItem = Main.item[i];
			if (worldItem.active && rectangle.Contains(worldItem.Center.ToPoint()))
			{
				NetMessage.SendData(160, toClient, -1, null, i);
			}
		}
	}

	public bool CanShimmer()
	{
		int shimmerEquivalentType = GetShimmerEquivalentType();
		if (ShimmerTransforms.IsItemTransformLocked(shimmerEquivalentType))
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (shimmerEquivalentType == 560 && Main.slimeRain)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if ((shimmerEquivalentType != 4986 || NPC.unlockedSlimeRainbowSpawn) && ShimmerTransforms.GetTransformToItem(shimmerEquivalentType) <= 0 && !ShimmerTransforms.IsItemDecraftableAndIsDecraftUnlocked(this) && !ItemID.Sets.CommonCoin[shimmerEquivalentType])
		{
			return makeNPC > 0;
		}
		return true;
	}

	public int FindDecraftAmount()
	{
		int decraftingRecipeIndex = ShimmerTransforms.GetDecraftingRecipeIndex(GetShimmerEquivalentType(forDecrafting: true));
		if (decraftingRecipeIndex < 0)
		{
			return -1;
		}
		return stack / Main.recipe[decraftingRecipeIndex].createItem.stack;
	}

	public int GetShimmerEquivalentType(bool forDecrafting = false)
	{
		if (forDecrafting && ItemID.Sets.ShimmerCountsAsItemForDecraft[type] != -1)
		{
			return ItemID.Sets.ShimmerCountsAsItemForDecraft[type];
		}
		if (ItemID.Sets.ShimmerCountsAsItem[type] != -1)
		{
			return ItemID.Sets.ShimmerCountsAsItem[type];
		}
		return type;
	}

	public void FixAgainstExploit()
	{
		if (ItemID.Sets.ItemsThatShouldNotBeInInventory[type])
		{
			TurnToAir();
			return;
		}
		if (stack > maxStack)
		{
			stack = maxStack;
		}
		if (prefix != 0 && !CanRollPrefix(prefix))
		{
			ResetPrefix();
		}
	}

64		public static bool IsItemDecraftableAndIsDecraftUnlocked(Item item)
65		{
66			if (item == null)
67			{
68				return false;
69			}
70			int decraftingRecipeIndex = GetDecraftingRecipeIndex(item.GetShimmerEquivalentType(forDecrafting: true));
71			if (IsRecipeIndexDecraftLocked(decraftingRecipeIndex))
72			{
73				return false;
74			}
75			if (decraftingRecipeIndex < 0)
76			{
77				return false;
78			}
79			return item.stack / Main.recipe[decraftingRecipeIndex].createItem.stack > 0;
80		}
81	
82		public static void UpdateRecipeSets()
83		{
84			RecipeSets.PostSkeletron = Utils.MapArray(Main.recipe, (Recipe r) => r.ContainsIngredient(154));
85			RecipeSets.PostGolem = Utils.MapArray(Main.recipe, (Recipe r) => r.ContainsIngredient(1101));
86		}

89		{

91			if (num > 0)
92			{
93				return num;
94			}
95			if (ContentSamples.ItemsByType[type].createTile == 139)

97				return ContentSamples.ItemsByType[type].placeStyle switch
98				{
99					90 => 5538, 
100					89 => 5579, 
101					97 => 5638, 
102					96 => 5639, 
103					_ => 576, 
104				};
105			}
106			if (type == 3461)
107			{
108				return GetLunarBrickTransformFromMoonPhase(Main.GetMoonPhase());
109			}
110			return 0;
111		}
112	
113		private static int GetLunarBrickTransformFromMoonPhase(MoonPhase moonPhase)
114		{
115			return moonPhase switch
116			{
117				MoonPhase.QuarterAtRight => 5407, 
118				MoonPhase.HalfAtRight => 5405, 
119				MoonPhase.ThreeQuartersAtRight => 5404, 
120				MoonPhase.Full => 5408, 
121				MoonPhase.ThreeQuartersAtLeft => 5401, 
122				MoonPhase.HalfAtLeft => 5403, 
123				MoonPhase.QuarterAtLeft => 5402, 
124				_ => 5406, 
125			};
126		}

38	const nextInt = (maxExclusive: number, rand: () => number): number => {
39	  const m = Math.floor(maxExclusive);
40	  return m <= 0 ? 0 : Math.floor(rand() * m);
41	};
42	
43	/** Luck.RollLuck（Luck.cs:5-16） */
44	export function rollLuck(luck: number, range: number, rand: () => number = Math.random): number {
45	  if (luck > 0 && rand() < luck) return nextInt(Math.floor(range / 2) + nextInt(range - Math.floor(range / 2), rand), rand);
46	  if (luck < 0 && rand() < -luck) return nextInt(range + nextInt(range, rand), rand);
47	  return nextInt(range, rand);
48	}
49	
50	/** Luck.RollBadLuck（:18-29） */
51	export function rollBadLuck(luck: number, range: number, rand: () => number = Math.random): number {
52	  if (luck > 0 && rand() < luck) return nextInt(range + nextInt(range, rand), rand);
53	  if (luck < 0 && rand() < -luck) return nextInt(Math.floor(range / 2) + nextInt(range - Math.floor(range / 2), rand), rand);
54	  return nextInt(range, rand);
55	}
56	
57	/** Luck.RollOnlyBadLuck（:31-38）：正运不受益 */
58	export function rollOnlyBadLuck(luck: number, range: number, rand: () => number = Math.random): number {
59	  if (luck < 0 && rand() < -luck) return nextInt(Math.floor(range / 2) + nextInt(range - Math.floor(range / 2), rand), rand);
60	  return nextInt(range, rand);
61	}
62	
63	/** Luck.RollBadLuckExtreme（:40-51） */
64	export function rollBadLuckExtreme(luck: number, range: number, rand: () => number = Math.random): number {
65	  if (luck > 0 && rand() < luck) return nextInt(range * 10, rand);
66	  if (luck < 0 && rand() < -luck) return nextInt(Math.floor(range / 10), rand);
67	  return nextInt(range, rand);
68	}
69

131	  addCoinLuck(distPx: number, coinAmount: number): void {

136	  /** 时序衰减（每 tick 调用；Main.dayRate 默认 1）：
137	   *  - 瓢虫 :28654-28670（正负分别向 0 收敛）
138	   *  - 钱币 UpdateCoinLuck :17956-17963（×0.9999^dayRate，<0.25 清零）
139	   *  - 碎镜 UpdateBrokenMirrorLuck :28700-28711 */
140	  update(dayRate = 1): void {
141	    if (this.ladyBugLuckTimeLeft > 0) {

143	    } else if (this.ladyBugLuckTimeLeft < 0) {

149	    }
150	    if (this.brokenMirrorBadLuckTime > 0) {

166	  ladyBugTouched(distPx: number, gold = false): void {

174	// ============ 火炬幸运扫描（Player.cs:17580-17720 窗口 + :17771-17939 计算 1:1） ============
175	// 窗口：玩家 Center tile ±40（81×81）。原版每 tick 只扫一行（luckyTorchCounter 逐行推进），
176	// 扫满 81 行才 ConsumeCountersAndCalculate；本仓按 15 tick 一整窗直扫（节流等价，语义同净效果）。
177	// 地表（position.Y ≤ worldSurface*16）或死亡 → 直接 Consume（torchLuck=0）。
178	
179	/** TorchID（Terraria.ID/TorchID.cs:84-131）——窗口内见到的火炬样式集索引 */
180	export const TORCH_STYLE = {
181	  Torch: 0, Blue: 1, Red: 2, Green: 3, Purple: 4, White: 5, Yellow: 6, Demon: 7,
182	  Cursed: 8, Ice: 9, Orange: 10, Ichor: 11, UltraBright: 12, Bone: 13, Rainbow: 14,
183	  Pink: 15, Desert: 16, Coral: 17, Corrupt: 18, Crimson: 19, Hallowed: 20,
184	  Jungle: 21, Mushroom: 22, Shimmer: 23,
185	} as const;
186	export const TORCH_STYLE_COUNT = 24;
187	
188	export interface LuckScanZones {
189	  dungeon: boolean; temple: boolean; snow: boolean; desert: boolean;
190	  jungle: boolean; glowshroom: boolean; corrupt: boolean; crimson: boolean; hallow: boolean;
191	  /** WorldGen.oceanDepths（SceneMetrics.zoneBeach 同式：y≤oceanLevel 且 x 距边 <380） */
192	  ocean: boolean;
193	}
194	
195	export interface LuckScanInput {
196	  store: TileStore;
197	  tx: number; ty: number;
198	  /** 地表以下（position.Y > worldSurface*16）——地表恒无火炬幸运 */
199	  belowSurface: boolean;
200	  zones: LuckScanZones;
201	  /** 手持火把 placeStyle（inventory[selectedItem].createTile==4 && placeStyle<24；无 = null） */
202	  heldTorchStyle: number | null;
203	}
204	
205	const TORCH_WINDOW = 40;          // num2（Player.cs:17595）
206	const GNOME_WINDOW_X = 84;        // SceneMetrics ZoneScanSize 半宽（(1920/16+49)/2 取整）
207	const GNOME_WINDOW_Y = 61;
208	
209	/** 内部 tile id：火把（vanilla sheet 4）/ 花园地精（vanilla sheet 567） */
210	const TORCH_TILE_ID = (() => {
211	  for (let i = 0; i < TILE_DEFS.length; i++) if (TILE_DEFS[i]?.vanilla?.sheet === 4) return i;
212	  return -1;
213	})();
214	const GNOME_TILE_ID = (() => {
215	  for (let i = 0; i < TILE_DEFS.length; i++) if (TILE_DEFS[i]?.vanilla?.sheet === 567) return i;
216	  return -1;
217	})();
218	
219	/** 地精在否（SceneMetrics.cs:597-600：_tileCounts[567]>0，169×124 窗口任一即真） */
220	function scanGnome(st: TileStore, tx: number, ty: number): boolean {
221	  if (GNOME_TILE_ID < 0) return false;
222	  const x0 = Math.max(0, tx - GNOME_WINDOW_X), x1 = Math.min(st.w - 1, tx + GNOME_WINDOW_X);
223	  const y0 = Math.max(0, ty - GNOME_WINDOW_Y), y1 = Math.min(st.h - 1, ty + GNOME_WINDOW_Y);
224	  for (let y = y0; y <= y1; y++) {
225	    for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x++) {
226	      const i = st.idx(x, y);
227	      if (st.flags[i] && st.type[i] === GNOME_TILE_ID) return true;
228	    }
229	  }
230	  return false;
231	}
232	
233	/** 扫描火炬窗口 + 地精窗口，算出 torchLuck 与 gnome 在否（1:1 净效果版） */
234	export function scanLuckScene(input: LuckScanInput): { torchLuck: number; hasGardenGnome: boolean } {
235	  const { store: st, tx, ty } = input;
236	  if (!input.belowSurface) {
237	    // TryRecalculatingTorchLuck :17592-17598：地表直接 ConsumeCounters → torchLuck=0
238	    return { torchLuck: 0, hasGardenGnome: scanGnome(st, tx, ty) };
239	  }
240	
241	  // 火炬窗口计数（:17604-17640；nearbyTorches 燃烧计数只喂 Torch God 触发，
242	  // 由 TorchGod.ts 自扫，此处不重复累计）
243	  const nearby = new Array<boolean>(TORCH_STYLE_COUNT).fill(false);
244	  let dryCoral = false;
245	  if (TORCH_TILE_ID >= 0) {
246	    const s0x = Math.max(10, tx - TORCH_WINDOW), s1x = Math.min(st.w - 10, tx + TORCH_WINDOW);
247	    const s0y = Math.max(10, ty - TORCH_WINDOW), s1y = Math.min(st.h - 10, ty + TORCH_WINDOW);
248	    for (let y = s0y; y <= s1y; y++) {
249	      for (let x = s0x; x <= s1x; x++) {
250	        const i = st.idx(x, y);
251	        if (!st.flags[i] || st.type[i] !== TORCH_TILE_ID || st.frameX[i] < 0 || st.frameY[i] < 0) continue;
252	        const style = Math.floor(st.frameY[i] / 22);
253	        if (style < TORCH_STYLE_COUNT) {
254	          nearby[style] = true;
255	          // :17629-17634：珊瑚炬非水中 → dryCoral（下文按"错群系"计负）
256	          if (style === TORCH_STYLE.Coral && (st.liquid[i] === 0 || st.liquidType[i] !== 1)) dryCoral = true;
257	        }
258	      }
259	    }
260	  }
261	  if (input.heldTorchStyle !== null && input.heldTorchStyle < TORCH_STYLE_COUNT) {
262	    nearby[input.heldTorchStyle] = true;   // UpdateTorchLuck_ConsumeCountersAndCalculate :17775-17778
263	  }
264	
265	  // ---- UpdateTorchLuck_ConsumeCountersAndCalculate（:17771-17939）----
266	  // num2=good（正确群系火炬加分项）num=bad（错群系火炬负分项）
267	  const z = input.zones;
268	  let good = 0;
269	  let bad = 0;
270	  if (!z.dungeon && !z.temple) {
271	    if (nearby[TORCH_STYLE.Ice]) { good += z.snow ? 1 : 0; bad += z.snow ? 0 : 1; }
272	    if (nearby[TORCH_STYLE.Desert]) { good += z.desert ? 1 : 0; bad += z.desert ? 0 : 1; }
273	    if (nearby[TORCH_STYLE.Coral]) {
274	      if (z.ocean) good += 1;          // :17813-17815 海洋深度带内 = 正确群系
275	      else if (dryCoral) bad += 1;     // :17816-17818 干珊瑚炬 = 错群系
276	    }
277	    if (nearby[TORCH_STYLE.Jungle]) { good += z.jungle ? 1 : 0; bad += z.jungle ? 0 : 1; }
278	    if (nearby[TORCH_STYLE.Mushroom]) { good += z.glowshroom ? 1 : 0; bad += z.glowshroom ? 0 : 1; }
279	    if (nearby[TORCH_STYLE.Corrupt]) { good += z.corrupt ? 1 : 0; bad += z.corrupt ? 0 : 1; }
280	    if (nearby[TORCH_STYLE.Cursed] && z.corrupt) good += 1;   // :17828-17830
281	    if (nearby[TORCH_STYLE.Crimson]) { good += z.crimson ? 1 : 0; bad += z.crimson ? 0 : 1; }
282	    if (nearby[TORCH_STYLE.Ichor] && z.crimson) good += 1;    // :17831-17833
283	    if (nearby[TORCH_STYLE.Hallowed]) { good += z.hallow ? 1 : 0; bad += z.hallow ? 0 : 1; }
284	    if (nearby[TORCH_STYLE.Bone] && !z.snow && !z.desert && !z.corrupt && !z.crimson && !z.hallow && !z.jungle) {
285	      good += 0.5;                                            // :17834-17836
286	    }
287	    if (nearby[TORCH_STYLE.Torch]) {
288	      if (z.snow) bad += 2;                                    // :17837-17841
289	      else if (z.desert || z.corrupt || z.crimson || z.hallow) bad += 0.5;
290	    }
291	  }
292	  let torchLuck = 0;
293	  if (good >= 1) torchLuck += 1;
294	  else if (good > 0) torchLuck += 0.5;
295	  if (bad >= 2) torchLuck += -1.5;

30	
31	  fixedUpdate(dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
32	    this.age++;
33	    // 寿命（10 分钟）
34	    if (this.age > 60 * 600) { this.dead = true; return; }
35	
36	    const player = (game as unknown as { player: Player }).player;
37	    let beingGrabbed = false;
38	    if (player && !player.dead && this.age > PICKUP_DELAY) {
39	      // 原版 GrabItems(Player.cs:34461-34524):hitbox 相交=直接拾取;
40	      // 否则玩家盒 ±42px(defaultItemGrabRange :2406)扩展盒相交=拉取；
41	      // 宝藏磁石(+94)/金戒指族(+12)扩展范围（Player.grabRangeBonus 聚合）
42	      const GRAB_RANGE = 42 + ((player as { grabRangeBonus?: number }).grabRangeBonus ?? 0)
43	        // 拾心药水（lifeMagnet）：红心单独扩大拾取范围
44	        + (this.pickup === 'heart' ? ((player as { heartGrabBonus?: number }).heartGrabBonus ?? 0) : 0)
45	        // 魔力磁石（manaMagnet：星 184/1735/1868 专用吸附，Player.cs:34495 专属拉取通道）
46	        + (this.pickup === 'star' ? ((player as { manaMagnetBonus?: number }).manaMagnetBonus ?? 0) : 0);
47	      const touching =
48	        this.x < player.x + player.w && this.x + this.w > player.x &&
49	        this.y < player.y + player.h && this.y + this.h > player.y;
50	      const inGrabRange =
51	        this.x < player.x + player.w + GRAB_RANGE && this.x + this.w > player.x - GRAB_RANGE &&
52	        this.y < player.y + player.h + GRAB_RANGE && this.y + this.h > player.y - GRAB_RANGE;
53	      if (touching) {
54	        // 心/星 pickup：直接回复并消失，不进背包（原版拾取即 Heal(20)/mana+100+ClearOut）
55	        if (this.pickup) {
56	          if (this.pickup === 'heart') {
57	            player.hp = Math.min(player.maxHp, player.hp + 20);
58	            game.addDamageNumber(player.cx, player.y, 20, false, '#40E080');
59	          } else {
60	            player.mana = Math.min(player.maxMana, player.mana + 100);
61	            game.addDamageNumber(player.cx, player.y, 100, false, '#4080FF');
62	          }
63	          this.dead = true;
64	          game.playSfx('pickup', 0.75); // 原版 SoundID 7 Grab
65	          return;
66	        }
67	        const before = this.stack;
68	        const left = player.inv.add(this.itemId, this.stack);
69	        if (left === 0) {
70	          this.dead = true;
71	          game.notifyInventoryChanged();
72	          // 铜币拾取用专属音效，其余走通用拾取
73	          // 音量略低（0.75）：拾取与挖掘声同时触发时两者都可闻，不被 Grab 盖住
74	          game.playSfx(ITEM_DEFS[this.itemId]?.key === 'coin_copper' ? 'coin' : 'pickup', 0.75);
75	          const def = ITEM_DEFS[this.itemId];
76	          if (def) game.showPickupLabel(def.key);
77	          return;
78	        }
79	        if (left !== before) game.notifyInventoryChanged();
80	        this.stack = left;
81	      } else if (inGrabRange) {
82	        // PullItem_Common(:34533-34584):每轴 0.45 步进、钳 4(水平含 player.vx),
83	        // 速度反向时附加 ×0.75 反拉——被拉取帧跳过瓦片碰撞(WorldItem.cs:587-597
84	        // else 分支仅 position+=velocity),物品穿墙飞向玩家
85	        beingGrabbed = true;

92	          if (this.vx > 0) this.vx -= SPEED * BACK;

95	          if (this.vy < MAXV) this.vy += SPEED;

101	      }
102	    }
103

/** 掉落物 → 原版物品 id（vi_NNN 前缀解析；原生 key 走稳定表反查） */
const NATIVE_ITEM_VID: Record<string, number> = {
  coin_copper: 71, coin_silver: 72, coin_gold: 73, coin_platinum: 74,
  gel: 23, torch: 8, lens: 236, stone_block: 3, dirt_block: 2, wood: 9,
  lesser_healing_potion: 28,
};
function dropVanillaId(d: ItemDrop): number {
  return slotVanillaId(d.itemId);
}

/** 内部 item id → 原版 item id（vi_<id>_ 键解析；原生 key 走 NATIVE_ITEM_VID） */
function slotVanillaId(itemId: number): number {
  const key = ITEM_DEFS[itemId]?.key ?? '';
  const vid = viIdFromKey(key);
  if (vid >= 0) return vid;
  return NATIVE_ITEM_VID[key] ?? -1;
}

  };

  accessory?: 'lucky_horseshoe' | 'feral_claws'; // 配饰效果

  value?: number;         // 钱币价值（铜币）

// 从 Terarria1456 Item.cs SetDefaults1..5 的 case 块提取 rare 字段 → src/data/vanilla-itemrare.json。
// 未列出的物品 rare=0（ResetStats 默认）。供 Tooltip 稀有度着色精确化（原版 GetItemNameColor 15 档）。
// 2026-08-11 增量：同时提取 value（含 sellPrice(p,g,s[,c]) 求值）→ vanilla-itemvalue.json
//（重铸费用 value×stack÷3 与商店定价用；vi_ 批次注册默认 value=1，权威值在此表）。
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';

const ROOT = '~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld';
const src = readFileSync(`${ROOT}/Terarria1456/Terraria/Item.cs`, 'utf8');
const RARE = {};
const VALUE = {};
const caseRe = /case (\d+):([\s\S]*?)(?=case \d+:|default:|\n\t\tbreak;)/g;
let m;
// sellPrice(platinum, gold, silver[, copper]) = p*1000000 + g*10000 + s*100 + c（Item.cs 同名方法）
const evalValue = (expr) => {
  const sp = expr.match(/sellPrice\((\d+(?:\s*,\s*\d+)*)\)/);
  if (sp) {
    const a = sp[1].split(',').map((x) => parseInt(x.trim(), 10));
    return (a[0] || 0) * 1000000 + (a[1] || 0) * 10000 + (a[2] || 0) * 100 + (a[3] || 0);
  }
  const lit = expr.match(/^(\d+)$/);
  return lit ? +lit[1] : null;
};
while ((m = caseRe.exec(src))) {
  const id = +m[1];
  const body = m[2].slice(0, 1200);
  const r = body.match(/\brare = (-?\d+);/);
  if (r) RARE[id] = +r[1];
  const v = body.match(/\bvalue = ([^;]+);/);
  if (v) {
    const val = evalValue(v[1].trim());
    if (val !== null) VALUE[id] = val;
  }
}
writeFileSync(`${ROOT}/game/src/data/vanilla-itemrare.json`, JSON.stringify(RARE));
writeFileSync(`${ROOT}/game/src/data/vanilla-itemvalue.json`, JSON.stringify(VALUE));
console.error(`rare 条目: ${Object.keys(RARE).length} / value 条目: ${Object.keys(VALUE).length}`);

Player.ts structure is clear. I need to add shimmer detection in this block (like inHoney), and effect handling:

3. AddBuff(353, 60) equivalent: our BuffType may not have 353. The debuff: shimmering=true, frozen (input locked), fallStart reset (no fall damage), invulnerable (Hurt returns 0 via AllowShimmerDodge), gravity×0.9. Then TryToShimmerUnstuck: timeShimmering accumulates +1 per tick while shimmering, ≥3600 (or ≥1200 + any input) → teleport to shimmer-free spot.

Wait — actually when does the player get the 353 buff? On touching shimmer (AddBuff(353,60) refreshed every frame while wet). The buff then makes them shimmering: they slowly sink (frozen, fall through??). Hmm — in vanilla, when you jump into shimmer you start "shimmering" — becoming transparent, sinking slowly through the liquid; when you reach the bottom/solid, buffTime set to 6 (when stuck in solid) or DelBuff when not in solid... wait reading again:

In UpdateBuffs for 353:
- shimmering = true; frozen = true; fallStart = position (no fall damage)
- if y > UnderworldLayer → DelBuff (shimmer doesn't work in hell)
- if shimmerWet → buffTime[j] = 60 (refresh), continue
- else (not wet): if overlapping any solid tile → buffTime = 6 (about to expire) else DelBuff (remove).

So: the shimmering state persists while in shimmer; once out of the liquid, if you're inside solid blocks (sinking through), the buff lasts 6 more ticks then expires — meaning the player passes through blocks? Hmm, actually shimmering doesn't disable tile collision directly in vanilla... The player sinks in shimmer liquid. The "unstuck" happens if stuck too long. Honestly the full vanilla behavior is complex (sinking through the bottom and teleporting up? No).

Real gameplay: jumping into shimmer grants the "Shimmered" debuff where you fall through solid blocks and keep falling until you either exit into an open space below or the debuff expires... no wait. Let me think about actual Terraria 1.4.4: When a player enters Shimmer, they get the Shimmered debuff, become unable to move, slowly sink. When they sink to the bottom and remain, after a while they're teleported back up to the top of the shimmer pool ("unstuck"). Actually I recall: players fall through blocks while shimmered and pop out where there's space. The `TryFindingShimmerFreeSpot` teleports them to a nearby spot without shimmer — this is the "return" mechanic when stuck for too long (60s) or pressing input for 20s.

The task says: "本仓玩家侧可做传送/治疗等核心效果" — do core effects: teleport + brief invulnerability. So implement:
- shimmerWet detection
- physics: light gravity + stronger swim/jump (in shimmer, jump out)

Teleport: find nearest shimmer-free spot near current position (search radius), teleport there with 1s? Actually vanilla teleports within ~60 tiles.

Also there's the fall-through behavior. Hmm — key mechanic: shimmering makes you fall through solid tiles (pass through). That's why "if overlapping solid → buffTime=6" else DelBuff: when you sink into the ground below the pool the buff keeps you going briefly.     for (const dent of this.entities.drops) {

      const def = ITEM_DEFS[d.itemId];
      if (!def) continue;
      const tx = Math.floor((d.x + d.w / 2) / TILE), ty = Math.floor((d.y + d.h / 2) / TILE);
      const dst = this.world.store;
      if (!dst.inBounds(tx, ty)) continue;
      const isTorch = def.tile === 'torch';
      const isCandle = def.tile === 'candle';
      if (isTorch || isCandle) {
        if (dst.liquid[dst.idx(tx, ty)] > 100) continue; // !wet
        this.lighting.addLight(tx, ty, 1, 0.95, 0.8);
        continue;
      }
      // 荧光棒族（vi id → 原版色；无 wet 门控）
      const vid = viIdFromKey(def.key);
      if (vid === 282) this.lighting.addLight(tx, ty, 0.7, 1, 0.8);
      else if (vid === 286) this.lighting.addLight(tx, ty, 0.7, 0.8, 1);
      else if (vid === 3002) this.lighting.addLight(tx, ty, 1.05, 0.95, 0.55);
      else if (vid === 75) this.lighting.addLight(tx, ty, 0.8, 0.7, 0.1);      // 坠落之星 :1719
      else if (vid === 183) this.lighting.addLight(tx, ty, 0.15, 0.45, 0.9);   // 陨石锭 :1715
    }
    // 实体点光（原版 Lighting.AddLight 的 NPC 侧调用）：AI 每 tick 写 lightRGB——

    // 魔化夜 crawler 抖动（:34492）；敌怪与小动物两桶同扫

## 👤 User(sidechain) · 2026-08-12T05:58:39.101Z

```
// 幸运度系统回归（Luck.cs 1:1 + RecalculateLuck 聚合 + 火炬/地精扫描）。
// 探针断言：
//  1. RollLuck 三态命中率（luck=0 基线 / 正运改掷 [N/2,N) / 负运恒不中）
//  2. RollOnlyBadLuck 正运无加成、负运收窄
//  3. LuckState 聚合公式数值（药水+灯笼+地精+火炬+装备叠加）
//  4. scanLuckScene 火炬窗口（冰炬=雪原正确群系 +1；错群系火炬负贡献钳 0；地精在窗）
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import {
  rollLuck, rollOnlyBadLuck, rollBadLuckExtreme, rollOnlyBadLuckExtreme,
  LuckState, scanLuckScene, TORCH_STYLE, type LuckScanZones,
} from '../src/stats/Luck';
import { TILE_DEFS } from '../src/data/tiles';
import type { TileStore } from '../src/world/TileStore';
import { Player } from '../src/entities/Player';
import { Inventory } from '../src/items/Inventory';
import { BuffType } from '../src/stats/Buffs';

const TRIALS = 200_000;
/** P(rollLuck(luck, N) === 0) 蒙特卡洛（大样本容差 10%） */
function zeroRate(fn: () => number): number {
  let hit = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < TRIALS; i++) if (fn() === 0) hit++;
  return hit / TRIALS;
}

describe('Luck.cs 掷骰三态（Luck.cs:5-60）', () => {
  it('RollLuck：luck=0 基线 1/N；luck=1 正运命中率约 +37%；luck=-1 负运约减半', () => {
    const base = zeroRate(() => rollLuck(0, 100));
    expect(base).toBeCloseTo(0.01, 3);
    const good = zeroRate(() => rollLuck(1, 100));
    // 正运必触发：Next(Next(50,100))，期望命中率 = ∫₅₀¹⁰⁰ (1/x dx)/50 ≈ ln2/50 ≈ 0.0139
    expect(good).toBeGreaterThan(base * 1.2);
    expect(good).toBeLessThan(0.03);
    const bad = zeroRate(() => rollLuck(-1, 100));
    // 负运：Next(Next(100,200))，期望命中率 ≈ ln2/100 ≈ 0.0069（≈ 基线减半，非恒不中）
    expect(bad).toBeGreaterThan(0.004);
    expect(bad).toBeLessThan(base * 0.85);
  });

  it('RollOnlyBadLuck：正运不受益（同基线）；负运收窄为 [N/2, N)', () => {
    const base = zeroRate(() => rollOnlyBadLuck(0, 100));
    const good = zeroRate(() => rollOnlyBadLuck(1, 100));
    expect(base).toBeCloseTo(0.01, 3);
    expect(Math.abs(good - base)).toBeLessThan(0.003);   // 正运与 0 等价
    const bad = zeroRate(() => rollOnlyBadLuck(-1, 100));
    expect(bad).toBeGreaterThan(base * 1.2);             // 负运下血月墓园怪更易刷
  });

  it('Extreme 两函数：正运 ×10 窗口（P0≈1/1000）/ 负运 ÷10 窗口（P0≈0.1）；OnlyBadLuckExtreme 非负运恒 -1', () => {
    // RollBadLuckExtreme 语义是"掷中小值=坏"：luck>0 → Next(N*10)（P0 变小）、luck<0 → Next(N/10)
    expect(zeroRate(() => rollBadLuckExtreme(1, 100))).toBeCloseTo(0.001, 3);
    expect(zeroRate(() => rollBadLuckExtreme(-1, 100))).toBeGreaterThan(0.05);
    let negOne = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < 1000; i++) if (rollOnlyBadLuckExtreme(0.5, 100) === -1) negOne++;
    expect(negOne).toBe(1000);
    let hit = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < 10_000; i++) if (rollOnlyBadLuckExtreme(-1, 100) === 0) hit++;
    expect(hit / 10_000).toBeCloseTo(0.1, 2);   // [0, N/10) 上命中 0 ≈ 10%
  });
});

describe('RecalculateLuck 聚合（Player.cs:28672-28697）', () => {
  it('各来源叠加数值：药水 0.3 + 灯笼 0.3 + 地精 0.2 + 火炬 0.2', () => {
    const p = new Player(0, 0, new Inventory());
    expect(p.luck).toBe(0);
    p.buffs.apply(BuffType.Luck, 900);                    // 档 3 → +0.3
    expect(p.luck).toBeCloseTo(0.3, 5);
    p.luckState.lanternsUp = true;                        // +0.3
    expect(p.luck).toBeCloseTo(0.6, 5);
    p.luckState.hasGardenGnomeNearby = true;              // +0.2
    p.luckState.torchLuck = 1;                            // +0.2（正确群系火炬满档）
    expect(p.luck).toBeCloseTo(1.0, 5);
  });

  it('装备路径基线：无幸运币/马掌 → 装备加成 0（hasLuck_* Player.cs:12629-12647）', () => {
    const p = new Player(0, 0, new Inventory());
    expect(p.luckEquipmentBonus).toBe(0);
    expect(p.luckPotionTier).toBe(0);
  });

```

   *  hover 下落无左右移时 wingTime 消耗减半（×0.5） */
  private wingMovement(wings: number, hoverDown: boolean, JS: number): void {
    if (wings === 4 && this.inputUp) {

        else { num2 = 0.95; num3 = 4.5; }
        num5 = 0.15; num4 = 1; num = 0.125;
        if (this.inputUp) {
          // TryingToHoverUp 附加推力（:21821-21838 / :21866-21883 同构）
          this.vy -= 0.4;
          if (this.vy > 0) this.vy -= 1;
          else if (this.vy > -JS) this.vy -= 0.2;

      }

      this.vy -= num;
      if (this.vy > 0) this.vy -= num2;

      if (this.vy < -JS * num3) this.vy = -JS * num3;

      this.wingTime -= halfCost ? 0.5 : 1;
    }
    this.fallStartY = null;
  }

  damage(dmg: number, srcX: number, srcY: number, knockback = true): boolean {
    if (this.iframes > 0 || this.dead) return false;
    const eq = this.equipStats;
    // 黑带/忍者大师装备 10% 闪避（Player.Hurt :9772 blackBelt 分支）
    if (eq.blackBelt && Math.random() < 0.1) return false;
    // 耐久药水(114)：endurance+0.1 → 受伤 ×0.9（Player.endurance 在 Hurt 结算）
    const endurance = this.buffs.has(BuffType.Endurance) ? 0.1 : 0;
    const reduction = Math.max(1, Math.round((dmg - this.defense * 0.5) * (1 - endurance)));
    this.hp -= reduction;
    // 十字项链/星星面纱：无敌帧翻倍（longInvince）
    this.iframes = PLAYER_IFRAME_TICKS * (eq.longInvince ? 2 : 1);
    this.sinceHurt = 0; // 受击打断自然回血
    // 甲虫攻击球受击掉一颗
    if (this.beetleOrbs > 0) this.beetleOrbs--;
    // 混乱之脑（:37488-37496：受击 1/6 → buff 321 + 困惑近敌；damage 无 game 参→脉冲标志）
    if (this.equipStats.boc && Math.random() < 1 / 6) this.bocPulse = 1;
    // 恐慌项链：受击触发 5 秒移速爆发（panicTime=300，PanicSpeedBoost :25034）
    if (eq.panic) this.panicTime = 300;
    // 魔力手铐（magicCuffs :37678）：受伤回蓝 = 原始伤害 1:1（防前数值）
    if (eq.magicCuffs) this.mana = Math.min(this.maxMana, this.mana + dmg);
    // 击退（钴护盾族 noKnockback 免疫）
    if (knockback && !eq.noKnockback) {
      const dir = this.cx < srcX ? -1 : 1;
      this.vx = dir * 3.4;
      this.vy = -3;
    }
    return true;
  }

      this.refillT = 0;
    }
    // 补气动画推进（补满后停留 REFILL_HOLD 再隐藏）
    if (this.refillT >= 0) {
      this.refillT += dt;
      if (this.refillT >= this.refillDur + 0.15) this.refillT = -1;
    }

    // 死亡等待重生（任何死法统一在此发声——溺水/岩浆/摔落/受击都经过这里）
    if (this.hp <= 0) {
      if (!this.dead) game.playSfx('pkilled');
      this.dead = true;
      return;
    }

    // 水平（敏捷 Buff 提速 + 装备移速（赫尔墨斯靴族 runSpeed/词缀 73-76）；蜂蜜比水更黏滞）
    const speedMult = this.buffs.moveSpeedMult * this.equipStats.moveMult * (this.inHoney ? 0.5 : 1);
    // 冰冻 Frozen(47,Player.cs:9749 frozen=true)：整帧封移动输入——跳跃/攀绳/矿车
    // 同读这些标志,一并归零(原版 Update 段 control* 清零语义)。帧末由 Game 重灌输入
    if (this.buffs.has(BuffType.Frozen)) { this.inputX = 0; this.inputJump = false; this.inputUp = false; this.inputDown = false; }
    const ix = this.inputX;
    let accel = PLAYER_WALK_ACCEL;
    if (!this.onGround && this.equipStats.wing && this.vy !== 0) {
      const ws = wingStatOf(this.equipStats.wing.logic);
      const hoverDownMove = this.inputDown && this.inputJump && ws.hover && this.wingTime > 0;
      accel *= hoverDownMove && ws.hover ? ws.hoverAccelMult : ws.accelMult;
    }
    if (ix !== 0) {
      this.vx += ix * accel * (this.inWater ? 0.6 : 1) * speedMult;
      this.facing = ix;
    } else {
      this.vx *= this.onGround ? PLAYER_FRICTION : PLAYER_AIR_FRICTION;
      if (Math.abs(this.vx) < 0.05) this.vx = 0;
    }
    let maxSpd = PLAYER_WALK_MAX * (this.inWater ? 0.55 : 1) * speedMult;
    // 翅膀空中横移上限覆盖（WingAirLogicTweaks cs:28843-48：AccRunSpeedOverride；
    // hover 下落态改用 DownHoverSpeedOverride 档）
    if (!this.onGround && this.equipStats.wing && this.vy !== 0) {
      const ws = wingStatOf(this.equipStats.wing.logic);
      const hoverDownMove = this.inputDown && this.inputJump && ws.hover && this.wingTime > 0;
      const spd = hoverDownMove && ws.hoverSpeed > 0 ? ws.hoverSpeed : ws.accSpeed;
      if (spd > maxSpd) maxSpd = spd;
    }
    this.vx = Math.max(-maxSpd, Math.min(maxSpd, this.vx));

    // 绳索攀爬(原版:身体中心格为绳(213/353/950-9)时无重力,上/下键攀爬,左右离绳)
    const st = world.store;
    const ropeTx = Math.floor((this.x + this.w / 2) / TILE);
    const ropeTy = Math.floor((this.y + this.h / 2) / TILE);
    const ropeHere = !!(st.inBounds(ropeTx, ropeTy) && st.flags[st.idx(ropeTx, ropeTy)]
      && TILE_DEFS[st.type[st.idx(ropeTx, ropeTy)]]?.rope);
    this.onRope = ropeHere;
    if (ropeHere && !this.inWater) {
      // 原版攀爬(Player.cs:17169-17212):上爬 vy>0 先阻尼×0.7,-3 以上每 tick -0.2
      // (之下 -0.02,下限 -8);下滑镜像(+0.2/+0.1,上限 maxFallSpeed);静止 vy*=0.7
      if (this.inputJump) {
        if (this.vy > 0) this.vy *= 0.7;
        this.vy -= this.vy > -3 ? 0.2 : 0.02;
        if (this.vy < -8) this.vy = -8;
      } else if (this.inputDown) {
        if (this.vy < 0) this.vy *= 0.7;
        this.vy += this.vy < 3 ? 0.2 : 0.1;
        if (this.vy > MAX_FALL_SPEED) this.vy = MAX_FALL_SPEED;
      } else {
        this.vy *= 0.7;
      }
      this.fallStartY = null; // 绳上不计摔伤
    } else
    // 跳跃 / 游泳
    if (this.inWater) {
      // ---- 水面行走（WaterWalkingBoots/LavaWaders 族，Player.WaterWalk 语义近似）----
      // 装备后落向液面时站在液面（vy 清零、可正常行走/起跳/重置空中能力）；
      // 按 ↓ 下潜。waterWalk2（水上漂靴）同效。
      if (canWaterWalk && !this.headUnderwater && this.vy >= 0 && !this.inputDown) {
        this.onWaterSurface = true;
        this.vy = 0;
        this.fallStartY = null;
        // 站在液面等价落地：重置多段跳/飞行/飞毯，允许起跳离水面
        this.extraJumps = [...this.equipStats.jumpOpts];
        const eqW = this.equipStats;
        this.wingTime = eqW.wing ? eqW.wing.time : 0;
        this.rocketTime = 7; this.rocketRelease = true; this.rocketDelay = 0;
        this.canCarpet = true;
        this.carpetTime = 300;
        if (this.inputJump && this.surfaceJumpCd <= 0) {
          this.vy = -PLAYER_JUMP_SPEED;
          this.jumpHold = PLAYER_JUMP_TICKS;
          this.surfaceJumpCd = 24;
        }
        if (this.surfaceJumpCd > 0) this.surfaceJumpCd--;
      } else {
        this.onWaterSurface = false;
        // 头部露出水面（踩水状态）→ 允许正常力度起跳跃上岸块（带冷却防连跳）
        if (this.inputJump && !this.headUnderwater) {
          if (this.surfaceJumpCd <= 0) {
            this.vy = -PLAYER_JUMP_SPEED;
            this.jumpHold = PLAYER_JUMP_TICKS;
            this.surfaceJumpCd = 24;
          }
      } else if (this.inputJump) {
        // 全浸没：游泳上浮（脚蹼族 accFlipper：提速 1.5×）
        const swimMult = (this.equipStats.flipper || this.buffs.has(BuffType.FlipperPotion)) ? 1.5 : 1;
        this.vy = Math.max(this.vy - 0.62 * swimMult, -4.4 * swimMult);
      }
      if (this.surfaceJumpCd > 0) this.surfaceJumpCd--;
      this.vy += GRAVITY * 0.3;
      this.vy = Math.max(-4.6, Math.min(3.0, this.vy));
      this.fallStartY = null;
      }
    } else {
      const gd = this.gravDir;
      if (this.inputJump && this.onGround) {
        // 跳跃速度 = 基础 + jumpSpeedBoost（蛙腿族 +1.6，Player.jumpSpeedBoost :22427）；
        // 重力倒置时方向取反（gravDir，Player.cs:3207 全部 vy 乘 gravDir 语义）
        this.vy = -(PLAYER_JUMP_SPEED + this.equipStats.jumpSpd) * gd;
        this.jumpHold = PLAYER_JUMP_TICKS;
      }
      if (this.inputJump && this.jumpHold > 0) {
        this.vy -= 0.22;
        this.jumpHold--;
      } else {
        this.jumpHold = 0;
      }
      // ---- 多段跳（RefreshDoubleJumps + JumpMovement 近似）：云朵/沙暴/暴雪/屁瓶族 ----
      // 空中再按跳（持续按住 + 段数剩余 + 未在急升中）→ 消费一段；各段速度原版梯度近似
      const eq = this.equipStats;
      if (this.onGround) this.extraJumps = [...eq.jumpOpts]; // 落地重置段数
      if (!this.onGround && this.inputJump && this.extraJumps.length > 0 && this.vy > -2 && this.extraJumpCd <= 0) {
        const opt = this.extraJumps.shift()!;
        const mult = opt === 'Fart' ? 1.28 : (opt === 'Sandstorm' || opt === 'Blizzard') ? 1.18 : 1.0;
        this.vy = -PLAYER_JUMP_SPEED * mult * (eq.jumpBoost ? 1.25 : 1);
        this.jumpHold = PLAYER_JUMP_TICKS;
        this.extraJumpCd = 8; // 防同帧连段
      }
      if (this.extraJumpCd > 0) this.extraJumpCd--;
      // ---- 翅膀/火箭靴/飞毯 1:1（Player.Update :26388-27092 + WingMovement :21746 +
      //      CarpetMovement :21568 + RocketBoots :26548-26628）----

  spawnDrop(x: number, y: number, itemKey: string, count?: number, vx?: number, vy?: number): import('./ItemDrop').ItemDrop | null;
  /** 对玩家造成伤害（attacker 用于荆棘反伤） */
  damagePlayer(dmg: number, srcX: number, srcY: number, attacker?: unknown): void;
  /** 伤害数字飘字 */
  addDamageNumber(x: number, y: number, amount: number, crit: boolean, color?: string): void;
  /** 弹幕砍 tileCut 物（Projectile.CutTiles 语义：箭扫过杂草/瓦罐 → Game 内部分流） */
  cutTile(x: number, y: number): void;
  /** 传送门弹 601 落门（PortalHelper.TryPlacingPortal + AddPortal；Game 实现入 props 桶） */
  placePortal?(x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, form: number): void;
  /** 敌人死亡 */
  onEnemyKilled(key: string, enemy?: import('../entities/Enemy').Enemy): void;
  /** 出生敌人 */
  spawnEnemy(key: string, x: number, y: number): void;
  /** 粒子 */
  spawnParticles(
    x: number, y: number, color: string, count: number, speed?: number,
    opts?: { life?: number; damp?: number; grav?: number; size?: number },
  ): void;
  /** 定向粒子爆发（烟花爆花/喷泉/马桶水花等原版 dust 带初速方向的场景；
   *  spawnParticles 为全向均匀发射，无法表达定向）。dir 为中心角、spread 为半散布，
   *  xMul/yMul 为原版轴向压缩（如 168 尘 y×0.75、马桶尘 x×0.5） */
  spawnBurst?(
    x: number, y: number, color: string, count: number, speed: number,
    dir: number, spread: number,
    opts?: { life?: number; damp?: number; grav?: number; xMul?: number; yMul?: number },
  ): void;
  /** 拾取物品后刷新 UI（掉落物实体调用） */
  notifyInventoryChanged(): void;
  /** 播放音效 */
  // 世界坐标声（原版 SoundEngine.PlaySound 带 x/y）：x/y 省略 = UI/玩家动作声不衰减
  playSfx(name: string, vol?: number, x?: number, y?: number): void;
  playSfxFiles(files: string[], vol?: number, x?: number, y?: number): void; // 怪物专属声效（原版文件名列表）
  /** 按原版文件名直播（无合成兜底；缓冲未就位时仅触发后台懒加载，本播静默） */
  playSfxFile?(file: string, vol?: number, x?: number, y?: number): void;
  /** 拾取物品时在主角旁飘出物品名 */
  showPickupLabel(itemKey: string): void;
  /** 掉落系统补充上下文（灵魂/钥匙/风筝等条件）：Game 实现，缺省则这些条件按 false */
  dropContext?(): import('../drops/NpcDrops').DropExtras;
  /** 月事件上下文（南瓜月/霜月 wave-gate 掉落条件）：Game 实现，缺省按无事件（恒 false） */
  moonEventCtx?(): { kind: 1 | 2; wave: number } | null;
  /** 环境判定（Game.scene，SceneMetrics 每 15t 以玩家为中心刷新；Boss 暴怒度/石巨人
   *  脱庙加成读 zoneJungle/zoneTemple——原版 Main.player[target].ZoneJungle/ZoneLihzhardTemple） */
  scene?: import('../world/SceneMetrics').SceneFlags;
}

GetShimmerEquivalentType / ShimmerTransforms.cs） ============\n\n/** Item.GetShimmerEquivalentType（Item.cs:49073-49086）：CountsAs 族等价计数 */\nexport function getShimmerEquivalentType(vid: number, undefined && c !== -1) return c;\n  return vid;\n}\n\n/** 音乐盒动态分支（ShimmerTransforms.cs:95-105：createTile==139 按 placeStyle）：\n *  90→5538 / 89→5579 / 97→5638 / 96→5639 / 其余→576（钢琴） */\nconst MUSIC_BOX_TRANSFORM: Record<number, number> = { 90: 5538, 89: 5579, 97: 5638, 96: 5639 };\n\n/** ShimmerTransforms.GetTransformToItem（:88-111）：表优先；音乐盒按 placeStyle；\n *  3461 月相砖需月相运行时（GAP）返回 0 */\nexport function getTransformToItem(vid: number): number {\n  const t = ITEM_TRANSFORM[vid];\n  if (t !== undefined && t > 0) return t;\n  const def = ITEM_DEFS[internalIdOfVanilla(vid)];\n  if (def?.tile === 'v_139_musicboxes' && def.placeStyle !== undefined) {\n    return MUSIC_BOX_TRANSFORM[def.placeStyle] ?? 576;\n  }\n  return 0;\n}\n\n/** ShimmerTransforms.IsItemTransformLocked（:33-40）：月后物品需已败月总 */\nexport function isItemTransformLocked(vid: number, downedMoonlord: boolean): boolean {\n  return !downedMoonlord && false): boolean {\n  if (isItemTransformLocked(vid, downedMoonlord)) return false;\n  return getTransformToItem(vid) > 0 || COMMON_COIN.has(vid);\n}\n\n/** 钱币→微光化币面额放大（WorldItem.cs:1791-1810）：铜 ×1 / 银 ×100 / 金 ×10000 /\n *  铂 stack 钳 1 后 ×1000000。返回入 coinLuck 的铜币面额（stack 一并放大） */\nexport function coinLuckAmount(vid: number, stack: number): number {\n  switch (vid) {\n    case 72: return stack * 100;\n    case 73: return stack * 10000;\n    case 74: return Math.min(stack, 1) * 1000000;\n    default: return stack; // 71 铜币 ×1\n  }\n}\n\n// ============ NPC 侧（NPC.GetShimmered，NPC.cs:92502-92584） ============\n\n/** NPCID.Sets.ShimmerTransformToNPC（→ NPC.Transform 保持实体） */\nexport function npcShimmerTransformTo(npcId: number): number {\n  const t = NPC_TRANSFORM[npcId];\n  return t !== undefined && t >= 0 ? t : -1;\n}\n\n/** NPCID.Sets.ShimmerTransformToItem（→ 掉微光化物品后消散） */\nexport function npcShimmerTransformToItem(npcId: number): number {\n  const t = NPC_TRANSFORM_ITEM[npcId];\n  return t !== undefined && t >= 0 ? t : -1;\n}\n\n/** NPCID.Sets.ShimmerTownTransform（城镇 NPC 微光变体，ai[0]=25） */\nexport function isNpcTownShimmerable(npcId: number): boolean {\n  return NPC_TOWN_TRANSFORM.has(npcId);\n}\n\n// ============ 玩家脱困传送（ShimmerHelper.FindSpotWithoutShimmer 近似） ============\n\n/** TryFindingShimmerFreeSpot（Player.cs:28414-28440）净效果版：以 (tx,ty) 为心，\n *  半径 r ∈ {1,3,…,59}（原版 num=60 循环步 2）找第一个「3 格高无实心无微光 +\n *  脚下可站（solid 或 solidTop）」的落点。找不到返回 null（调用方保持原位） */\nexport function findShimmerFreeSpot(\n  st: TileStore, tx: number, ty: number,\n): { x: number; y: number } | null {\n  const clear = (x: number, y: number): boolean => {\n    for (let j = y - 2; j <= y; j++) {\n      if (!st.inBounds(x, j)) return false;\n      const i = st.idx(x, j);\n      if (st.isSolid(x, j) || (st.liquid[i] > 0 && st.liquidType[i] === 4)) return false;\n    }\n    return st.inBounds(x, ty + 1);\n  };\n  const standable = (x: number, y: number): boolean => {\n    if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false;\n    return st.isSolid(x, y) || st.isPlatform(x, y);\n  };\n  for (let r = 1; r < 60; r += 2) {\n    // 先纵后横：紧邻圈优先正下/正上，再向两侧展开（原版即按距离近者优先语义）\n    for (let dy = -r; dy <= r; dy += 2) {\n      for (let dx = -r; dx <= r; dx += 2) {\n        if (Math.max(Math.abs(dx), Math.abs(dy)) < r - 1) continue; // 只扫外环\n        const x = tx + dx, y = ty + dy;\n        if (!st.inBounds(x, y) || !standable(x, y + 1)) continue;\n        if (clear(x, y)) return { x, y };\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n\n/** 世界坐标落点 → 玩家传送位（ShimmerUnstuck :28400-28402：spot + (0,-2) 像素） */\nexport function shimmerTeleportPos(spot: { x: number; y: number }): { x: number; y: number } {\n  return { x: spot.x * this.cx, player.cy - this.cy);\n      player.luckState.addCoinLuck(dist, amount);\n      this.stack = 0;\n      this.dead = true;\n      return;\n    }\n    if (target > 0) {\n      const iid = internalIdOfVanilla(target);\n      if (iid >= 0) {\n        this.itemId = iid;       // SetDefaults(target) 原堆叠保留（:1813-1817）\n        this.shimmered = true;    // 上浮漂浮\n        this.shimmerTime = 1;\n        this.vx *= 0.1; this.vy *= 0.1;\n        game.playSfx('shimmerSplash', 0.75, this.cx, this.cy);\n      }\n    }\n  }"

524:  fixedUpdate(dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
  fixedUpdate(dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
    const world = game.world;
    if (this.iframes > 0) this.iframes--;
    if (this.useTime > 0) this.useTime--;

    // Buff tick：自然回复 / 环境 DoT（负值=持续伤害,原版 lifeRegen 路径:无视防御与无敌帧）
    const buffHeal = this.buffs.tick(dt);
    if (buffHeal > 0 && this.hp > 0) this.hp = Math.min(this.maxHp, this.hp + buffHeal);
    else if (buffHeal < 0 && this.hp > 0) {
      this.hp = Math.max(0, this.hp + buffHeal);
      if (this.hp <= 0) this.lastDamageCause = { kind: 'default' }; // DoT 致死=默认死亡包
    }
    // 恐慌项链加速窗口倒计时

    this.dead = true; return;
  }
}

85:  fixedUpdate(dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
    this.animT++;
    if (this.iframes > 0) this.iframes--;
    const world = game.world;

    // 被绑待救援：原地不动（仅重力落定），解救在 Game.talkToNearbyNpc
    if (this.bound) {
      this.vx = 0;
      this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + GRAVITY, MAX_FALL_SPEED);
      moveAndCollide(this, world, this.vx, this.vy);
      return;
    }

    let wantDir = 0;
    // 对话中站定并面向玩家（原版 AI_007 NPC.cs:53721-53742：talkNPC==本体 →
    // ai[0]=0 停走、ai[1]=300、direction 指向玩家——杜绝"聊着聊着走开丢对话"）
    if (this.talking) {
      const p = (game as unknown as { player: Player }).player;
      if (p) this.facing = p.cx < this.cx ? -1 : 1;
      this.vx *= 0.6;
      this.wanderT = 0;
    } else if (!this.stationary && world.clock.isDay) {
      // 白天家门口附近游走;leashHome 者离 home 超过 ~10 格时折返(守卫老人不离地牢)
      if (this.leashHome && this.home
        && Math.abs(this.cx - (this.home.x * TILE + 8)) > 10 * TILE) {
        wantDir = Math.sign(this.home.x * TILE + 8 - this.cx);
      } else {
        this.wanderT--;
        if (this.wanderT <= 0) {
          this.wanderT = 90 + Math.random() * 180;
          this.facing = Math.random() < 0.5 ? -1 : 1;
          if (Math.random() < 0.35) this.facing = 0;
        }
        wantDir = this.facing;
      }
    } else if (this.home) {
      // 夜间回家
      const dx = this.home.x * TILE + 8 - this.cx;
      if (Math.abs(dx) > TILE) wantDir = Math.sign(dx);
    }

    this.vx += wantDir * 0.06;
    this.vx = Math.max(-0.8, Math.min(0.8, this.vx));

      // 原版驻留刹车（NPC.cs:54096-54106）：不走路时每 tick ±0.1 主动减速。

      // → 向右滑行显示朝左贴图（偶发"太空步"）
      if (this.vx > 0.1) this.vx -= 0.1;
      else if (this.vx < -0.1) this.vx += 0.1;
      else this.vx = 0;

    if (this.hitWall) this.vx *= 0.5;
    if (wantDir !== 0) this.facing = wantDir;

  constructor(dayLengthMs: number) { this.dayLengthMs = dayLengthMs; }
  tick(dtMs: number) {
    this.timeOfDay += dtMs / this.dayLengthMs;
    while (this.timeOfDay >= 1) { this.timeOfDay -= 1; this.dayCount++; }
  }
  get isDay(): boolean { return this.timeOfDay > 0.25 && this.timeOfDay < 0.75; }

  }

  constructor(w: number, h: number, seed: number, name = '新世界') {

    this.explored = new Uint8Array(w * h);

  /** 从 worker 数据包重建（buffer 已 transfer 移交，零拷贝包装）。
   *  explored 未包含在包内（saveGame 不持久化、新生成/读档均全零）时按全零分配 */
  static fromPacket(p: import('../workers/protocol').WorldPacket): World {
    const w = new World(p.w, p.h, p.seed, p.name);
    w.store = new TileStore(p.w, p.h, p.buf);
    w.explored = p.buf.explored ? new Uint8Array(p.buf.explored) : new Uint8Array(p.w * p.h);
    w.spawnX = p.spawnX; w.spawnY = p.spawnY;
    w.groundLevel = p.groundLevel; w.rockLevel = p.rockLevel; w.lavaLine = p.lavaLine;
    w.crimson = p.crimson; w.dungeonX = p.dungeonX; w.dungeonY = p.dungeonY; w.jungleX = p.jungleX;

// 链路:iceQueenAI → shootE（hostile:true Arrow）→ Arrow.fixedUpdate →
// hitPlayer → GameHooks.damagePlayer → Player.damage（防御减算 + iframes）。
// 此前全仓敌弹只判敌人桶,该链在 hitPlayer 一环断开——本探针锁定回归。
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { Enemy } from '../src/entities/Enemy';
import { Player } from '../src/entities/Player';
import { Inventory } from '../src/items/Inventory';
import { World } from '../src/world/World';
import { TILE } from '../src/core/constants';
import type { GameHooks } from '../src/entities/types';

const W = 160, H = 120, FLOOR = 100;

function makeWorld(): World {
  const w = new World(W, H, 7, 'icequeen-sim');
  const st = w.store;
  w.groundLevel = 50;
  for (let x = 0; x < W; x++) {
    st.setTile(x, FLOOR, 1);
    st.setTile(x, FLOOR + 1, 1);
  }
  w.clock.timeOfDay = 0.9; // 夜间（isDay = 0.25..0.75 之外）
  return w;
}

describe('冰女王敌弹命中玩家（端到端）', () => {
  it('FrostWave 348 命中玩家:玩家掉血 + damagePlayer 结算', () => {
    const world = makeWorld();
    const p = new Player(70 * TILE, 96 * TILE, new Inventory());
    const hp0 = p.hp;
    const hits: number[] = [];
    const enemies: Enemy[] = [];
    const projectiles: Array<{ fixedUpdate: (dt: number, g: GameHooks) => void; dead: boolean }> = [];
    const em = {
      nextId: 1,
      add: (e: unknown, bucket: string) => {
        const ent = e as { id: number };
        ent.id = em.nextId++;
        if (bucket === 'enemies') enemies.push(e as Enemy);
        else projectiles.push(e as typeof projectiles[number]);
      },
    };
    const hooks = {
      world, player: p, entities: em,
      enemies: () => enemies,
      critters: () => [],
      spawnDrop: () => null,
      damagePlayer: (dmg: number, sx: number, sy: number) => { hits.push(dmg); p.damage(dmg, sx, sy); },
      addDamageNumber: () => {},
      cutTile: () => {},
      onEnemyKilled: () => {},
      spawnEnemy: () => {},
      spawnParticles: () => {},
      notifyInventoryChanged: () => {},
      playSfx: () => {},
      playSfxFiles: () => {},
    } as unknown as GameHooks;

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { World } from '../src/world/World';
import { Player } from '../src/entities/Player';
import { Inventory } from '../src/items/Inventory';
import { TILE_BY_KEY } from '../src/data/tiles';

// 黏滞 tile（Collision.StickyTiles + Player.cs:22650-22740）
const fakeHooks = (w: World) => ({
  world: w,
  spawnDrop: () => null,
  addDamageNumber: () => {},
  playSfx: () => {},
  spawnParticles: () => {},
  onInventoryChanged: () => {},
});

describe('蛛网/蜂蜜块黏滞（StickyTiles 1:1）', () => {
  it('蛛网内：下落钳 1 且 ×0.3 缓沉、水平 ×0.6/0.85 阻尼、禁跳', () => {
    const w = new World(40, 40, 42, 'web');
    const st = w.store;
    const WEB = TILE_BY_KEY['v_51_cobweb']!;
    // 20,20 放一列 4 格蛛网
    for (let y = 18; y <= 21; y++) st.setTileSilent(20, y, WEB);
    const p = new Player(20 * 16, 17 * 16, new Inventory());
    p.vy = 8; p.vx = 3; p.jumpHold = 10; // 高速下落 + 横移 + 跳跃中
    p.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, fakeHooks(w) as never);
    expect(p.vy).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1 * 0.3 + 0.36); // 钳 1 后 ×0.3 + 本 tick 重力
    expect(p.jumpHold).toBe(0);
    expect(p.vx).toBeLessThan(1.5); // 水平阻尼明显
  });

  it('无黏滞 tile 时不受影响', () => {
    const w = new World(40, 40, 42, 'open');
    const p = new Player(20 * 16, 17 * 16, new Inventory());
    p.vy = 8;
    p.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, fakeHooks(w) as never);
    expect(p.vy).toBeGreaterThan(4); // 正常重力累积
  });

  it('蜂蜜块：Y 缓沉生效但不禁跳', () => {
    const w = new World(40, 40, 42, 'honey');
    const st = w.store;
    const HONEY = TILE_BY_KEY['v_229_honey_block']!;
    for (let y = 18; y <= 21; y++) st.setTileSilent(20, y, HONEY);
    const p = new Player(20 * 16, 17 * 16, new Inventory());
    p.vy = 8; p.jumpHold = 10;
    p.inputJump = true; // 按住跳：正常每 tick -1（=9）；若被黏滞清零会是 0
    p.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, fakeHooks(w) as never);
    expect(p.vy).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1 * 0.3 + 0.36);
    expect(p.jumpHold).toBe(9); // 自然衰减 1，未被黏滞清零（type != 229 才清）
  });

  it('撕网目标 1:1（Collision.StickyTiles 列优先 cs:3399）：最左重叠列优先于更上行的网', () => {
    const w = new World(40, 40, 42, 'open');
    const st = w.store;
    const WEB = TILE_BY_KEY['v_51_cobweb']!;
    for (let x = 18; x <= 26; x++) st.setTileSilent(x, 30, 2); // 宽地板
    // 两张网：左列低位 (20,29) 与右列高位 (21,27)——行优先会先撕 (21,27)，

    const drops: Array<{ x: number; y: number }> = [];

 "content": "# 原版微光实体转化系统（补齐清单 #5）\n\n三层转化 1:1（Terarria1456 权威）：\n- **掉落物**（WorldItem.cs:806-840 Shimmering / :1781-1931 GetShimmered）：\n  ItemDrop 入微光（liquidType 4，顶上一格采样）shimmerTime +0.01/t >0.9 触发：\n  ① 钱币 71-74 → 面额放大（银×100/金×10000/铂钳1×1e6）→ `player.luckState.addCoinLuck`\n  （Luck.ts coinLuck 数值位就此接通，<1000px 距离门）；② ShimmerTransformToItem 有表 →\n  换目标物品原堆叠+`shimmered` 上浮（vy -0.05 钳 -4、速度≥0.2 不可拾 Player.cs:34466）；\n  不可转化物（如坠落之星 75）正常沉底不转化。wet 判定已排除微光（Collision.cs:1418）。\n- **玩家**（Player.cs:27420 触发 / :11381 buff353 / :37591 免伤 / :28377 脱困）：\n  浸微光（y<lavaLine）→ shimmering：frozen 封输入 + 免摔伤 + **damage() 直接 false** +\n  gravity 0.15 轻浮大跳（非微光态）；timeShimmering ≥3600 或 ≥1200 且有输入 →\n  `findShimmerFreeSpot`（切比雪夫圈 1..59 步 2，勿用步 2 内环——奇偶会漏一半格子）传送\n  spot+(0,-2)px + 40t 无敌帧。地狱层内不生效（:11391）。\n- **NPC**（NPC.cs:92502 GetShimmered）：Enemy 浸微光 90t（shimmerTransparency 0.9）→\n  雕像产怪消散 / ShimmerTransformToNPC transformTo / ShimmerTransformToItem 掉微光化\n  物品后消散；TownNPC 入微光 → `shimmered=true` + 回家（ai[0]=25 净效果）。\n\n**转化表来源**：`game/tools/extract-shimmer.mjs` 解析 ItemID.cs:84/86/88/90/1098 +\nNPCID.cs:4839/4841/4843 → `game/src/data/vanilla-shimmer.json`（312 物品对 + 114 NPC 对\n+ 15 NPC→物品 + 29 城镇变体 + CommonCoin/PostMoonlord）；运行时 `game/src/stats/Shimmer.ts`\n（含音乐盒 createTile=139 placeStyle 动态分支 + 内部↔原版 id 互查）。\n\n**GAP 登记**（Shimmer.ts 头注释有全清单）：decraft 反 craft（需配方引擎+RecipeSets 骷髅\n王/石巨人锁）、makeNPC 放生、4986 彩虹史莱姆解锁/560 史莱姆雨、3461 月相砖、微光视觉\n（shimmerTransparency 半透明/dust309）、Critter 小动物入微光、联机 145/146 同步。\n\n回归：`tests/shimmer.test.ts` 12 探针（表抽查/luck 接通/掉落物端到端/玩家浸入+脱困/\n地狱层门）。注意 vitest 全量有并行会话 flake（luck-system 聚合与 fishing Bobber.ts 中途\n态、重 worldgen 用例满载偶发），单跑均绿。\n"
}
```

2. 玩家（Player.cs:27420/11381/37591/28377）：浸微光 → shimmering（y<lavaLine 门）= frozen 封输入 + 免摔伤 + **damage() 直接免伤** + 轻浮大跳（gravity 0.15）；timeShimmering ≥3600 或 ≥1200 且有输入 → `findShimmerFreeSpot` 传送脱困 + 40t 无敌帧。地狱层内不生效。
3. NPC（NPC.cs:92502）：Enemy 浸微光 90t → 雕像产怪消散 / NPC→NPC transformTo / NPC→物品掉微光化物后消散；TownNPC 入微光 → shimmered 标记 + 回家（ai[0]=25 净效果）。

**登记 GAP**（Shimmer.ts 头注释全清单）：decraft 反 craft（需配方引擎+RecipeSets 三锁）、makeNPC 放生、4986 彩虹史莱姆/560 史莱姆雨、3461 月相砖、微光视觉（半透明/dust309）、Critter 小动物入微光、联机 145/146 同步、TownNPC 变体贴图/对话差分。

**验证**：`tests/shimmer.test.ts` 12 探针全绿（表抽查/luck 接通/掉落物端到端/玩家浸入+脱困/地狱层门）；tsc 本任务文件零新增（现存 15 错均为并行会话 UnderworldPass/Dart/_ghost 中间态）；vitest 全量 880+ 绿，余下失败单跑均过（luck-system 聚合与 fishing Bobber 为并行会话编辑中文件，重 worldgen 用例满载偶发）。另补了 `Buff.Weak.Desc` 中英文 l10n 键（并行会话缺键曾阻塞全仓 vitest 启动门）。记忆已登记 `vanilla-shimmer-port.md`。
